mysql> SELECT order_id,
    ->        total_retail_price,
    ->        (cost_price_per_unit * quantity_ordered) AS total_cost,
    ->        (total_retail_price - (cost_price_per_unit * quantity_ordered)) AS profit

mysql> SELECT customer_status,
    ->        ROUND(AVG(DATEDIFF(delivery_date, order_date)), 1) AS avg_delivery_days
    -> FROM orders
    -> GROUP BY customer_status;

mysql> SELECT product_id,
    ->        SUM(quantity_ordered) AS total_units_sold,
    ->        SUM(total_retail_price) AS total_revenue
    -> FROM orders
    -> GROUP BY product_id
    -> ORDER BY total_units_sold DESC
    -> LIMIT 10;
